    window.loadScript = (url, { charset = 'UTF-8', ...attributes } = {}, location = 'head') => {
      const existingScript = document.querySelector('script[src="' + url + '"]');
      if (existingScript) {
        return;
      }

      const script = document.createElement('script');
      script.src = url;
      script.type = 'text/javascript';

      script.async = true;
      script.charset = charset;

      if (attributes) {
        Object.entries(attributes).forEach(([key, value]) => {
          script.setAttribute(key, value);
        });
      }

      if (location === 'head') {
        document.head.appendChild(script);
      } else {
        document.body.appendChild(script);
      }

      return script;
    };

uid="niONQ" component-url="/_astro/GoogleAnalytics.Buaiy_s2.js" component-export="GoogleAnalytics" renderer-url="/_astro/client.CpQ9otmg.js" props="{&quot;title&quot;:[0,&quot;Introducing Precursor: detecting agentic behavior with continuous client-side signals&quot;],&quot;canonical&quot;:[0,&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/introducing-precursor&quot;],&quot;info&quot;:[0,{&quot;id&quot;:[0,&quot;6ePF6Vl7bAXNNpdFDoFbqs&quot;],&quot;title&quot;:[0,&quot;Introducing Precursor: detecting agentic behavior with continuous client-side signals&quot;],&quot;slug&quot;:[0,&quot;introducing-precursor&quot;],&quot;excerpt&quot;:[0,&quot;Precursor, our new continuous behavioral validation engine for bot management, offers visibility into how humans and bots actually interact across the full user journey. By turning session-level behavior into bot detection signals, it identifies advanced automation with higher precision â while reducing friction for legitimate users.&quot;],&quot;featured&quot;:[0,false],&quot;html&quot;:[0,&quot;&lt;p&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Bot mitigation is an adversarial game: attackers adapt, defenders respond, and the cycle continues. At Cloudflare, we stay ahead by combining visibility across our global network with signals from the client-side environment. At the network level, we analyze over 1 trillion requests per day to understand reputation, patterns, and anomalies across more than 20% of the web. On the client side, weâve pushed detection deeper with &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/turnstile/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Cloudflare Turnstile&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, which has evolved from a &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/turnstile-private-captcha-alternative/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;CAPTCHA replacement&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; to a risk-based managed challenge that adapts the amount of friction needed to verify the user is authentic.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Today, Turnstile runs nearly 3 billion times per day on some of the most sensitive endpoints on the Internet, helping verify users at key moments like login, signup, and checkout. This improves protection on the most important areas of customer applications, but still leaves limited visibility into the rest of the application â how humans and bots actually interact across the full user journey.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;This is the visibility gap weâre closing today with our launch of &lt;b&gt;Precursor&lt;/b&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;introducing-precursor\&quot;&gt;Introducing Precursor&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#introducing-precursor\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/cloudflare-challenges/precursor\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Precursor&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; is a client-side, session-based verification system, built with privacy in mind, that uses dynamically injected JavaScript to continuously collect behavioral signals as visitors interact with your application. These signals are processed and incorporated into Cloudflareâs bot protection in real time, allowing us to continuously distinguish human traffic from automated or agentic traffic.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;This extends the client-side detections offered by a &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/cloudflare-challenges/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Challenge&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; to your entire web application. Precursor is an optional complement to Turnstile â both are features of our Enterprise Bot Management. \n\nThis user-journey-based detection is powerful because modern automation is increasingly capable of appearing legitimate in short bursts. Bots can execute JavaScript, use real browser environments, and pass individual CAPTCHAs without raising suspicion. What remains difficult to replicate is consistent human behavior over time.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Precursor is built to capture that layer of interaction, turning behavior itself into a reliable signal for detecting fraud and abuse. By evaluating behavior across an entire session, Precursor adds significantly more signal to each decision. This improves detection precision, making it easier to distinguish real users from automation without relying on aggressive Challenges. For legitimate users, Precursor means fewer unnecessary interruptions. For bot developers, it raises the cost of operating automation by requiring them to simulate a full session. This is significantly harder to build, more expensive to maintain, and far less reliable to operate at scale.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;to-err-is-human\&quot;&gt;To err is humanÂ &lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#to-err-is-human\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;When a bot developer tries to make a mouse movement look human, they usually add &lt;a href=\&quot;https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gaussian_noise\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Gaussian noise&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; or uniform random delays. But human movement isn&amp;#39;t just &amp;quot;noisy,&amp;quot; it is also constrained by physics:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;ul&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Wrist pivot:&lt;/b&gt; A human mouse movement is often an arc, limited by the range of the wrist and the rotation of the forearm.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Cognitive load:&lt;/b&gt; There is a measurable delay between a human seeing a checkbox and clicking it.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Hand tremor:&lt;/b&gt; Even the steadiest human hand oscillates at a physiological tremor frequency.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;p&gt;Bots, by contrast, often behave in ways that give them away. They move in linear interpolations or mathematically ideal BÃ©zier curves. They click with a precision that humans could never replicate. And even when they do manage to simulate human error, there is a rhythm to human movements that can only be seen by examining an entire session.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Mouse movement is just one example of the signals Precursor evaluates, but it illustrates the difference clearly. Below is an example of a mouse automation library interacting with a site. You can see how the mouse moves in perfectly straight lines, always returns to an origin, and reacts with the same velocity.Â &lt;/p&gt;&lt;div style=\&quot;position: relative; padding-top: 56.25%;\&quot;&gt;\n  &lt;iframe\n    src=\&quot;https://customer-eq7kiuol0tk9chox.cloudflarestream.com/a593f96c06c93ce0c14d4ae3a8bc09e8/iframe?muted=true&amp;preload=true&amp;loop=true&amp;autoplay=true&amp;poster=https%3A%2F%2Fcustomer-eq7kiuol0tk9chox.cloudflarestream.com%2Fa593f96c06c93ce0c14d4ae3a8bc09e8%2Fthumbnails%2Fthumbnail.jpg%3Ftime%3D%26height%3D600\&quot;\n    loading=\&quot;lazy\&quot;\n    style=\&quot;border: none; position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; height: 100%; width: 100%;\&quot;\n    allow=\&quot;accelerometer; gyroscope; autoplay; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture;\&quot;\n    allowfullscreen=\&quot;true\&quot;\n  &gt;&lt;/iframe&gt;\n&lt;/div&gt;\n&lt;p&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Now, contrast that with a human navigating the same site: you see irregular paths, small corrections and overshoots, and variations in speed, timing, and direction.Â &lt;/p&gt;&lt;div style=\&quot;position: relative; padding-top: 56.25%;\&quot;&gt;\n  &lt;iframe\n    src=\&quot;https://customer-eq7kiuol0tk9chox.cloudflarestream.com/bad8784fa2242e6b49428a007b0e3a03/iframe?muted=true&amp;preload=true&amp;loop=true&amp;autoplay=true&amp;poster=https%3A%2F%2Fcustomer-eq7kiuol0tk9chox.cloudflarestream.com%2Fbad8784fa2242e6b49428a007b0e3a03%2Fthumbnails%2Fthumbnail.jpg%3Ftime%3D%26height%3D600\&quot;\n    loading=\&quot;lazy\&quot;\n    style=\&quot;border: none; position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; height: 100%; width: 100%;\&quot;\n    allow=\&quot;accelerometer; gyroscope; autoplay; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture;\&quot;\n    allowfullscreen=\&quot;true\&quot;\n  &gt;&lt;/iframe&gt;\n&lt;/div&gt;\n&lt;p&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Individually, these interactions might look plausible. But over the course of a session, these patterns diverge in ways that are difficult to fake. Precursor is designed to capture and evaluate these behavioral signatures as they develop over a visitorâs interaction with an application.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;how-precursor-works\&quot;&gt;How Precursor works&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#how-precursor-works\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;To evaluate behavior over time, Precursor continuously collects interaction data on the client and builds a session-level view of activity for that site.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;1. Injection and collection layer&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;When Precursor is enabled on your application, Cloudflare automatically injects a lightweight script into HTML responses from your site as they pass through our network, with no additional configuration, network connections, or third-party embedding required. The injected Precursor bundle is compact, obfuscated, and assembled dynamically for each response. The bundle is designed to not interfere with any additional page logic of the hosted web application.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;The script attaches lightweight event listeners to capture interaction signals such as pointer movement, keyboard activity, focus changes, and visibility. These events are serialized into a compact format and buffered in memory. At regular intervals, the buffered data is sent back to the evaluation layer for analysis.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;2. Evaluation layer&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;On the edge server, incoming Precursor payloads are deserialized into behavioral inputs. A dispatcher runs a roster of evaluators on the input data. Each evaluator reads the Precursor streams it cares about and can raise signals into the shared detection registry.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Evaluators are designed to cross-reference data. For example, they confirm that pointer activity correlates with page visibility duration, or that keyboard events only fire when a text field is focused. This stream of information is then consolidated into individual signals that are used for weighting detections.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;3. Session integration&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Precursor data is session-scoped, meaning it accumulates throughout a session. Session scoping is important because it means a bot cannot reset its behavioral signature by refreshing the page or starting over with a new challenge. The system also feeds session metadata into downstream detection layers for additional shadow-mode heuristics and session analysis, predicted vs. actual completion, and session delinquency heuristics. These edge-side observations are logged for detection improvement purposes and to adjust the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/bots/concepts/bot-score/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;bot score&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; of a session.Â &lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;4. Privacy by design&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Precursor was designed to collect signals that help to distinguish human patterns from automated and abusive patterns. \n\nThe event listeners capture the minimum information needed to be a useful signal for detecting automation and abuse. For example, keyboard activity is captured as timing and rhythm, not as the actual keys pressed. In addition, behavioral signals are evaluated as aggregate patterns rather than individual actions and are consumed internally by Cloudflare&amp;#39;s bot detection systems; they are not exposed to customer dashboards or tied to user accounts, login identities, or persistent profiles.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Taken together, this allows Precursor to maintain a continuously evolving evaluation of behavior, maximizing precision while minimizing the friction on good users.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;per-session-analytics\&quot;&gt;Per-session analytics&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#per-session-analytics\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;To support this new layer of detection, we are introducing session-based views in Security Analytics. These dashboards shift the perspective from individual requests to full visitor journeys. You can now answer questions like:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;ol&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;What does a typical session look like on my site?&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;Where do sessions diverge from expected behavior?&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;Which sessions show signs of automation over time?&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;/ol&gt;\n          &lt;figure class=\&quot;kg-card kg-image-card\&quot;&gt;\n          &lt;Image src=\&quot;https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/7HGyTpEcRUXpf3oeEV2uCM/1b6b635c945db2d2cf7b6d03970aec10/BLOG-3362_image2.png\&quot; alt=\&quot;BLOG-3362 image2\&quot; class=\&quot;kg-image\&quot; width=\&quot;1999\&quot; height=\&quot;866\&quot; loading=\&quot;lazy\&quot;/&gt;\n          &lt;/figure&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;sup&gt;&lt;i&gt;Use Security Analytics to explore session-based views for your bot management traffic.&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/sup&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;These analytics now capture information that per-request analytics canât âÂ  especially the behavior that occurs between requests. Precursor feeds directly into existing systems like bot score, challenge decisions, and security rules, so you benefit from this added context immediately.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;whats-next\&quot;&gt;Whatâs next&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#whats-next\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Precursor is the foundation for extending bot detection across the entire application. We are continuing to expand the range and depth of behavioral signals for security, how session-level insights influence our bot management protections, and new ways to visualize and act on session data. As bots evolve, detection needs to move beyond isolated checkpoints and into the full flow of user activity.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;get-started\&quot;&gt;Get started&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#get-started\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Precursor is rolling out now and can be enabled directly from your Cloudflare dashboard. Precursor will be free to use until our GA release later this year. Getting started is simple: &lt;a href=\&quot;https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/:zone/security/settings\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;turn Precursor on&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; for your zone and choose how strictly you want to verify sessions. You can run it in a low-friction mode to observe behavior in the background, or require a fully verified session by enforcing Challenges if a session doesnât already exist.Â &lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Once enabled, Precursor begins enhancing your existing bot defenses immediately, with no changes required to your application. If you&amp;#39;re already using Bot Management or Turnstile, Precursor extends those protections beyond Challenges and into the rest of the session. Enable Precursor to extend detection across the full user session, including the activity between moments you already component-export="Post" renderer-url="/_astro/client.CpQ9otmg.js" props="{&quot;post&quot;:[0,{&quot;id&quot;:[0,&quot;6ePF6Vl7bAXNNpdFDoFbqs&quot;],&quot;title&quot;:[0,&quot;Introducing Precursor: detecting agentic behavior with continuous client-side signals&quot;],&quot;slug&quot;:[0,&quot;introducing-precursor&quot;],&quot;excerpt&quot;:[0,&quot;Precursor, our new continuous behavioral validation engine for bot management, offers visibility into how humans and bots actually interact across the full user journey. By turning session-level behavior into bot detection signals, it identifies advanced automation with higher precision â while reducing friction for legitimate users.&quot;],&quot;featured&quot;:[0,false],&quot;html&quot;:[0,&quot;&lt;p&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Bot mitigation is an adversarial game: attackers adapt, defenders respond, and the cycle continues. At Cloudflare, we stay ahead by combining visibility across our global network with signals from the client-side environment. At the network level, we analyze over 1 trillion requests per day to understand reputation, patterns, and anomalies across more than 20% of the web. On the client side, weâve pushed detection deeper with &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/turnstile/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Cloudflare Turnstile&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, which has evolved from a &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/turnstile-private-captcha-alternative/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;CAPTCHA replacement&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; to a risk-based managed challenge that adapts the amount of friction needed to verify the user is authentic.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Today, Turnstile runs nearly 3 billion times per day on some of the most sensitive endpoints on the Internet, helping verify users at key moments like login, signup, and checkout. This improves protection on the most important areas of customer applications, but still leaves limited visibility into the rest of the application â how humans and bots actually interact across the full user journey.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;This is the visibility gap weâre closing today with our launch of &lt;b&gt;Precursor&lt;/b&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;introducing-precursor\&quot;&gt;Introducing Precursor&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#introducing-precursor\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/cloudflare-challenges/precursor\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Precursor&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; is a client-side, session-based verification system, built with privacy in mind, that uses dynamically injected JavaScript to continuously collect behavioral signals as visitors interact with your application. These signals are processed and incorporated into Cloudflareâs bot protection in real time, allowing us to continuously distinguish human traffic from automated or agentic traffic.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;This extends the client-side detections offered by a &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/cloudflare-challenges/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Challenge&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; to your entire web application. Precursor is an optional complement to Turnstile â both are features of our Enterprise Bot Management. \n\nThis user-journey-based detection is powerful because modern automation is increasingly capable of appearing legitimate in short bursts. Bots can execute JavaScript, use real browser environments, and pass individual CAPTCHAs without raising suspicion. What remains difficult to replicate is consistent human behavior over time.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Precursor is built to capture that layer of interaction, turning behavior itself into a reliable signal for detecting fraud and abuse. By evaluating behavior across an entire session, Precursor adds significantly more signal to each decision. This improves detection precision, making it easier to distinguish real users from automation without relying on aggressive Challenges. For legitimate users, Precursor means fewer unnecessary interruptions. For bot developers, it raises the cost of operating automation by requiring them to simulate a full session. This is significantly harder to build, more expensive to maintain, and far less reliable to operate at scale.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;to-err-is-human\&quot;&gt;To err is humanÂ &lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#to-err-is-human\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;When a bot developer tries to make a mouse movement look human, they usually add &lt;a href=\&quot;https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gaussian_noise\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Gaussian noise&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; or uniform random delays. But human movement isn&amp;#39;t just &amp;quot;noisy,&amp;quot; it is also constrained by physics:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;ul&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Wrist pivot:&lt;/b&gt; A human mouse movement is often an arc, limited by the range of the wrist and the rotation of the forearm.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Cognitive load:&lt;/b&gt; There is a measurable delay between a human seeing a checkbox and clicking it.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;Hand tremor:&lt;/b&gt; Even the steadiest human hand oscillates at a physiological tremor frequency.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;p&gt;Bots, by contrast, often behave in ways that give them away. They move in linear interpolations or mathematically ideal BÃ©zier curves. They click with a precision that humans could never replicate. And even when they do manage to simulate human error, there is a rhythm to human movements that can only be seen by examining an entire session.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Mouse movement is just one example of the signals Precursor evaluates, but it illustrates the difference clearly. Below is an example of a mouse automation library interacting with a site. You can see how the mouse moves in perfectly straight lines, always returns to an origin, and reacts with the same velocity.Â &lt;/p&gt;&lt;div style=\&quot;position: relative; padding-top: 56.25%;\&quot;&gt;\n  &lt;iframe\n    src=\&quot;https://customer-eq7kiuol0tk9chox.cloudflarestream.com/a593f96c06c93ce0c14d4ae3a8bc09e8/iframe?muted=true&amp;preload=true&amp;loop=true&amp;autoplay=true&amp;poster=https%3A%2F%2Fcustomer-eq7kiuol0tk9chox.cloudflarestream.com%2Fa593f96c06c93ce0c14d4ae3a8bc09e8%2Fthumbnails%2Fthumbnail.jpg%3Ftime%3D%26height%3D600\&quot;\n    loading=\&quot;lazy\&quot;\n    style=\&quot;border: none; position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; height: 100%; width: 100%;\&quot;\n    allow=\&quot;accelerometer; gyroscope; autoplay; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture;\&quot;\n    allowfullscreen=\&quot;true\&quot;\n  &gt;&lt;/iframe&gt;\n&lt;/div&gt;\n&lt;p&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Now, contrast that with a human navigating the same site: you see irregular paths, small corrections and overshoots, and variations in speed, timing, and direction.Â &lt;/p&gt;&lt;div style=\&quot;position: relative; padding-top: 56.25%;\&quot;&gt;\n  &lt;iframe\n    src=\&quot;https://customer-eq7kiuol0tk9chox.cloudflarestream.com/bad8784fa2242e6b49428a007b0e3a03/iframe?muted=true&amp;preload=true&amp;loop=true&amp;autoplay=true&amp;poster=https%3A%2F%2Fcustomer-eq7kiuol0tk9chox.cloudflarestream.com%2Fbad8784fa2242e6b49428a007b0e3a03%2Fthumbnails%2Fthumbnail.jpg%3Ftime%3D%26height%3D600\&quot;\n    loading=\&quot;lazy\&quot;\n    style=\&quot;border: none; position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; height: 100%; width: 100%;\&quot;\n    allow=\&quot;accelerometer; gyroscope; autoplay; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture;\&quot;\n    allowfullscreen=\&quot;true\&quot;\n  &gt;&lt;/iframe&gt;\n&lt;/div&gt;\n&lt;p&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Individually, these interactions might look plausible. But over the course of a session, these patterns diverge in ways that are difficult to fake. Precursor is designed to capture and evaluate these behavioral signatures as they develop over a visitorâs interaction with an application.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;how-precursor-works\&quot;&gt;How Precursor works&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#how-precursor-works\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;To evaluate behavior over time, Precursor continuously collects interaction data on the client and builds a session-level view of activity for that site.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;1. Injection and collection layer&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;When Precursor is enabled on your application, Cloudflare automatically injects a lightweight script into HTML responses from your site as they pass through our network, with no additional configuration, network connections, or third-party embedding required. The injected Precursor bundle is compact, obfuscated, and assembled dynamically for each response. The bundle is designed to not interfere with any additional page logic of the hosted web application.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;The script attaches lightweight event listeners to capture interaction signals such as pointer movement, keyboard activity, focus changes, and visibility. These events are serialized into a compact format and buffered in memory. At regular intervals, the buffered data is sent back to the evaluation layer for analysis.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;2. Evaluation layer&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;On the edge server, incoming Precursor payloads are deserialized into behavioral inputs. A dispatcher runs a roster of evaluators on the input data. Each evaluator reads the Precursor streams it cares about and can raise signals into the shared detection registry.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Evaluators are designed to cross-reference data. For example, they confirm that pointer activity correlates with page visibility duration, or that keyboard events only fire when a text field is focused. This stream of information is then consolidated into individual signals that are used for weighting detections.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;3. Session integration&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Precursor data is session-scoped, meaning it accumulates throughout a session. Session scoping is important because it means a bot cannot reset its behavioral signature by refreshing the page or starting over with a new challenge. The system also feeds session metadata into downstream detection layers for additional shadow-mode heuristics and session analysis, predicted vs. actual completion, and session delinquency heuristics. These edge-side observations are logged for detection improvement purposes and to adjust the &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/bots/concepts/bot-score/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;bot score&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; of a session.Â &lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;4. Privacy by design&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Precursor was designed to collect signals that help to distinguish human patterns from automated and abusive patterns. \n\nThe event listeners capture the minimum information needed to be a useful signal for detecting automation and abuse. For example, keyboard activity is captured as timing and rhythm, not as the actual keys pressed. In addition, behavioral signals are evaluated as aggregate patterns rather than individual actions and are consumed internally by Cloudflare&amp;#39;s bot detection systems; they are not exposed to customer dashboards or tied to user accounts, login identities, or persistent profiles.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Taken together, this allows Precursor to maintain a continuously evolving evaluation of behavior, maximizing precision while minimizing the friction on good users.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;per-session-analytics\&quot;&gt;Per-session analytics&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#per-session-analytics\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;To support this new layer of detection, we are introducing session-based views in Security Analytics. These dashboards shift the perspective from individual requests to full visitor journeys. You can now answer questions like:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;ol&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;What does a typical session look like on my site?&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;Where do sessions diverge from expected behavior?&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;Which sessions show signs of automation over time?&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;/ol&gt;\n          &lt;figure class=\&quot;kg-card kg-image-card\&quot;&gt;\n          &lt;Image src=\&quot;https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/7HGyTpEcRUXpf3oeEV2uCM/1b6b635c945db2d2cf7b6d03970aec10/BLOG-3362_image2.png\&quot; alt=\&quot;BLOG-3362 image2\&quot; class=\&quot;kg-image\&quot; width=\&quot;1999\&quot; height=\&quot;866\&quot; loading=\&quot;lazy\&quot;/&gt;\n          &lt;/figure&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;sup&gt;&lt;i&gt;Use Security Analytics to explore session-based views for your bot management traffic.&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/sup&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;These analytics now capture information that per-request analytics canât âÂ  especially the behavior that occurs between requests. Precursor feeds directly into existing systems like bot score, challenge decisions, and security rules, so you benefit from this added context immediately.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;whats-next\&quot;&gt;Whatâs next&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#whats-next\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Precursor is the foundation for extending bot detection across the entire application. We are continuing to expand the range and depth of behavioral signals for security, how session-level insights influence our bot management protections, and new ways to visualize and act on session data. As bots evolve, detection needs to move beyond isolated checkpoints and into the full flow of user activity.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;get-started\&quot;&gt;Get started&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#get-started\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Precursor is rolling out now and can be enabled directly from your Cloudflare dashboard. Precursor will be free to use until our GA release later this year. Getting started is simple: &lt;a href=\&quot;https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/:zone/security/settings\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;turn Precursor on&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; for your zone and choose how strictly you want to verify sessions. You can run it in a low-friction mode to observe behavior in the background, or require a fully verified session by enforcing Challenges if a session doesnât already exist.Â &lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Once enabled, Precursor begins enhancing your existing bot defenses immediately, with no changes required to your application. If you&amp;#39;re already using Bot Management or Turnstile, Precursor extends those protections beyond Challenges and into the rest of the session. Enable Precursor to extend detection across the full user session, including the activity between moments you already The idea: for each origin behind your site, Cloudflare picks the single best upper-tier data center to route through, based on real-time latency. Flip one switch, and we find the fastest path from our network to your origin.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;That works as long as an origin IP lives in one fixed place. Public cloud origins usually don&amp;#39;t. They sit behind anycast or regional unicast front ends, so one origin IP can look equally close to a dozen Cloudflare data centers at once â and the latency probes have nothing to lock onto. Smart Tiered Cache handles this the safe way: when there&amp;#39;s no clear winner, it falls back to several upper tiers. Nothing breaks. You just lose the thing that made a single closest tier worth it, which is cache efficiency.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Smart Tiered Cache for Public Cloud Regions fixes this by letting you provide a cloud region hint. With that hint, Cloudflare can map public cloud origins to the right region and select better primary and fallback upper tiers, even when the origin IP itself looks anycast or ambiguous.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;we-made-our-most-popular-tiered-cache-topology-smarter\&quot;&gt;We made our most popular tiered cache topology smarter&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#we-made-our-most-popular-tiered-cache-topology-smarter\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Since it was launched, Smart Tiered Cache has become the most popular tiered cache topology among Cloudflare customers. Itâs available to all plans, for free.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Much of our work aims to continually improve it. Over time, weâve extended Smart Tiered Cache to handle more origin architectures, including:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;ul&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;November 2024&lt;/b&gt;: &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/changelog/post/2024-11-20-smart-tiered-cache-for-r2/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Smart Tiered Cache for R2&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;: We taught Smart Tiered Cache to automatically select the closest upper tier to where the R2 bucket actually lives, reducing latency with zero configuration.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;b&gt;January 2025&lt;/b&gt;: &lt;a href=\&quot;https://developers.cloudflare.com/changelog/post/2025-01-08-smart-tiered-cache-for-load-balancing/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Smart Tiered Cache for Load Balancing&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;: We extended Smart Tiered Cache to select a single optimal upper tier for an entire Load Balancing pool, so all origins in the pool share the same cache, improving hit ratios.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;p&gt;Each of these improvements has shared a common goal: &lt;b&gt;understand the customerâs origin infrastructure and automatically do the best thing for that infrastructure.&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;While weâve been improving this system for a while, customers still had a common frustration: Smart Tiered Cache did not work when an origin is behind an anycast or regional unicast network, because this architecture prevented us from knowing where the origin is located. And this wasnât an edge case, either. Origins hosted on public cloud providers behind anycast IPs are a growing slice of the Internet.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Today, weâre closing that gap for origins hosted on AWS, GCP, Azure, and Oracle Cloud.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;why-anycast-cloud-origins-are-different\&quot;&gt;Why anycast cloud origins are different&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#why-anycast-cloud-origins-are-different\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Smart Tiered Cache works by measuring the latency from each Cloudflare data center to the originâs IP address. The data center with the lowest latency becomes the upper tier: the single point through which all cache misses funnel on their way to your origin. By concentrating cache misses at one data center, you get higher cache hit ratios, fewer connections to your origin, and lower latency on origin pulls. This works well when the origin has a fixed, unicast IP address that can be reliably probed.&lt;/p&gt;\n          &lt;figure class=\&quot;kg-card kg-image-card\&quot;&gt;\n          &lt;Image src=\&quot;https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/1pLDhUXVPPshpZr9zeP4bb/cecf1530616ab1d6097597d5d26039a7/BLOG-3328_image6.png\&quot; alt=\&quot;BLOG-3328 image6\&quot; class=\&quot;kg-image\&quot; width=\&quot;1454\&quot; height=\&quot;572\&quot; loading=\&quot;lazy\&quot;/&gt;\n          &lt;/figure&gt;&lt;p&gt;Many cloud providers use &lt;a href=\&quot;https://www.cloudflare.com/learning/cdn/glossary/anycast-network/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;anycast&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; or regional unicast networking for their load balancers, front-end services, and regional ingress points. When we probe these IPs, the origin appears to be âcloseâ to many data centers simultaneously. That is because the IP address represents the cloud providerâs front end, not a single physical origin location. Different Cloudflare data centers may reach different nearby cloud edges for the exact same IP, and the provider then carries the request across its own network to the actual backend. So Smart Tiered Cache cannot confidently pick one best upper tier.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;In practice, this could result in hairpin traffic across continents, adding a whole extra round trip. Say your origin sits in Singapore, behind an anycast IP from a cloud provider. Because of how anycast works, our Chicago data center might show the lowest probe latency to that IP. Smart Tiered Cache would then select Chicago as the upper tier. The result: a request from an end user in Asia hits a nearby Cloudflare data center, gets routed cross-continent to the upper tier in Chicago, and Chicago fetches from the origin back in Singapore, crossing the ocean twice. That hairpinning adds hundreds of milliseconds of latency, and it&amp;#39;s one of the most consistently reported issues from customers with cloud-hosted origins.&lt;/p&gt;\n          &lt;figure class=\&quot;kg-card kg-image-card\&quot;&gt;\n          &lt;Image src=\&quot;https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/2hRyeNvGKp3XKYPUNqHitI/d4ad50d4fb977614b84b2d0a418ba19b/BLOG-3328_image5.png\&quot; alt=\&quot;BLOG-3328 image5\&quot; class=\&quot;kg-image\&quot; width=\&quot;1370\&quot; height=\&quot;487\&quot; loading=\&quot;lazy\&quot;/&gt;\n          &lt;/figure&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;sup&gt;&lt;i&gt;An example of hairpinning is when traffic is routed to an upper tier in Chicago only to fetch data from an origin in Singapore, resulting in an unnecessary cross-continental round trip.&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/sup&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;To address this unnecessary back-and-forth, Smart Tiered Cache learned to detect anycast origins with a constraint from physics: the speed of light. We measure probe latencies from multiple checkpoint data centers around the world to the origin. If the combined latencies from two checkpoint data centers are faster than what light in fiber could physically travel between the two, the origin must be answering from multiple locations, not one. That means it&amp;#39;s anycast.&lt;/p&gt;\n          &lt;figure class=\&quot;kg-card kg-image-card\&quot;&gt;\n          &lt;Image src=\&quot;https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/6ieQUciGC1WPjrbBuNGXVo/1656fc30c7fcc0b2cd925dfe8133ad55/BLOG-3328_image7.png\&quot; alt=\&quot;BLOG-3328 image7\&quot; class=\&quot;kg-image\&quot; width=\&quot;1999\&quot; height=\&quot;841\&quot; loading=\&quot;lazy\&quot;/&gt;\n          &lt;/figure&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;sup&gt;&lt;i&gt;We detect anycast origins by comparing probe latencies from multiple Cloudflare data centers. If two paths are faster than physically possible for a single origin location, the origin is likely answering from multiple places.&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/sup&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;When Smart Tiered Cache detects an anycast origin, it plays it safe: it won&amp;#39;t pin that IP to a single upper tier. Instead, it falls back to a tiered cache topology with multiple upper tiers. Tiered caching still works, but spreading traffic across multiple tiers instead of one means more requests reach the origin. For some setups thatâs a fine trade. But if you want one upper tier close to an origin that lives on a public cloud behind anycast IPs, there hasn&amp;#39;t been a good option â until now.Â &lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;tell-us-the-region\&quot;&gt;Tell us the region&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#tell-us-the-region\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;From the Cloudflare dashboard, go to &lt;b&gt;Caching &amp;gt; Tiered Cache&lt;/b&gt; &amp;gt; &lt;a href=\&quot;https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/:zone/caching/tiered-cache\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Origin Configuration&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. Find your origin IP, click &amp;quot;Set Region Hint,&amp;quot; and tell us the cloud region (for example, aws:us-east-1 or gcp:europe-west1). Smart Tiered Cache takes over from there. Note that, on the dashboard, region hints can only be set for origins whose IPs we&amp;#39;ve detected as anycast.&lt;/p&gt;\n          &lt;figure class=\&quot;kg-card kg-image-card\&quot;&gt;\n          &lt;Image src=\&quot;https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/1rX4q6d0GT5rfgfwmbh1nR/c95630fc56263c957ba8a8ec1271e343/BLOG-3328_image1.png\&quot; alt=\&quot;BLOG-3328 image1\&quot; class=\&quot;kg-image\&quot; width=\&quot;1640\&quot; height=\&quot;1370\&quot; loading=\&quot;lazy\&quot;/&gt;\n          &lt;/figure&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;sup&gt;&lt;i&gt;On the Tiered Cache page, go to the Origin Configuration table and click the edit icon next to an origin IP to set its region hint.&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/sup&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;You can set hints one IP at a time, or bulk-edit cloud regions for all your origin IPs at once. Beyond the dashboard, the same configuration is available via the API and through Terraform, so you can integrate it into your existing infrastructure-as-code workflows.&lt;/p&gt;\n          &lt;figure class=\&quot;kg-card kg-image-card\&quot;&gt;\n          &lt;Image src=\&quot;https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/01LGSHThxPazryOTxcKXQl/5da5187915393d27c2add6f8c350ee8c/BLOG-3328_image3.png\&quot; alt=\&quot;BLOG-3328 image3\&quot; class=\&quot;kg-image\&quot; width=\&quot;1620\&quot; height=\&quot;1100\&quot; loading=\&quot;lazy\&quot;/&gt;\n          &lt;/figure&gt;&lt;p&gt;We&amp;#39;re launching with AWS, GCP, Azure, and Oracle Cloud, with more providers coming.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;how-smart-tiered-cache-for-public-cloud-regions-works\&quot;&gt;How Smart Tiered Cache for Public Cloud Regions works&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#how-smart-tiered-cache-for-public-cloud-regions-works\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;Every few hours, we fetch the latest IP range files from each supported cloud provider. These files map every cloud region to its current set of IP prefixes, so when a provider adds, removes, or reassigns a subnet, we pick it up.&lt;/p&gt;\n          &lt;figure class=\&quot;kg-card kg-image-card\&quot;&gt;\n          &lt;Image src=\&quot;https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/5BB4tvNq2pOzOVsZARvebS/718bcc5c8b2ff42f4bf6afdc6e860c5b/BLOG-3328_image4.png\&quot; alt=\&quot;BLOG-3328 image4\&quot; class=\&quot;kg-image\&quot; width=\&quot;997\&quot; height=\&quot;333\&quot; loading=\&quot;lazy\&quot;/&gt;\n          &lt;/figure&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;sup&gt;&lt;i&gt;Smart Tiered Cache for Public Cloud system diagram&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/sup&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;We match those subnets against our upper tier database, which is built from continuous latency probing refreshed every 15 minutes. For each cloud region, each matching subnet contributes a weighted vote based on its current upper-tier assignment. The upper tier with the strongest signal becomes the regionâs primary upper tier. Primary and fallback always come from different points of presence (PoPs), so losing one PoP can&amp;#39;t take out both.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Some regions don&amp;#39;t have enough probe data, for example, perhaps the new regionâs cloud provider is still rolling out, or the region has no origin onboarded to Cloudflare yet â so thereâs nothing to vote on. We fall back to geography: the closest of our Tier 1 PoPs. As origins come online and probe data builds up, the region quietly switches from that geographic guess to the option backed by real data.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h3 id=\&quot;try-it-now-and-whats-next\&quot;&gt;Try it now, and what&amp;#39;s next&lt;/h3&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#try-it-now-and-whats-next\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;All this means that the work of selecting the optimal region for your cache â the constant probing, the algorithmic choice of each region&amp;#39;s best upper tier, the geographic fallbacks, the failover across PoPs â runs on our side. Your job is selecting the region hint.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;If your anycast origin sits on a public cloud, you can turn this on now. In the dashboard, go to Caching &amp;gt; Tiered Cache &amp;gt; &lt;a href=\&quot;https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/:zone/caching/tiered-cache\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;Origin Configuration&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. Find your origin IP, click Set Region Hint, and pick your region.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Next up, weâre expanding to more providers, and continuing to teach Smart Tiered Cache to recognize more origin setups and pick the right path on its own. hints.&quot;],&quot;imgPreview&quot;:[0,&quot;https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/3yZo3xtQ9NVNwhlgaE9cD6/b952e26f57dada17482993be816f6770/Improving_Smart_Tiered_Cache_for_public_cloud_regions-OG.png&quot;]}],&quot;publicly_index&quot;:[0,true]}],[0,{&quot;id&quot;:[0,&quot;5wMIF1Oa7DES2YNRpqiVB0&quot;],&quot;title&quot;:[0,&quot;Why we cannot wait for better post-quantum signature algorithms&quot;],&quot;slug&quot;:[0,&quot;ml-dsa-will-have-to-do&quot;],&quot;excerpt&quot;:[0,&quot;NIST is advancing nine new post-quantum signature algorithms as potential candidates for future standardization. We take a closer look at all of them, and argue that while they are in the works and show great potential, we should use ML-DSA for now â the best one currently available. &quot;],&quot;featured&quot;:[0,false],&quot;html&quot;:[0,&quot;&lt;p&gt;RSA and ECC, cryptographic algorithms that weâve all relied on for decades, are &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/the-quantum-menace/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;vulnerable&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; to the attack of sufficiently advanced quantum computers. Such quantum computers do not exist yet, but they seem to be coming &lt;a href=\&quot;https://scottaaronson.blog/?p=9718\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;sooner&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; than expected. Luckily, the solution is already available: migrate to ML-KEM encryption and ML-DSA signatures, which are designed to be resistant to quantum attack. They were standardized &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/nists-first-post-quantum-standards/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;in 2024&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; by the U.S. National Institute of Standards and Technology (NIST) after an eight-year open international competition.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;The migration to post-quantum cryptography is in full swing now. At the time of writing, the majority of traffic handled by Cloudflare is already using ML-KEM encryption, and is thus secured against the threat to data posed by &lt;a href=\&quot;https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harvest_now,_decrypt_later\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;harvest-now-decrypt-later&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; attacks. But encryption is only one part of the equation: to be fully secure against quantum computers capable of breaking classical cryptography, we aim to deploy post-quantum signatures to protect authentication systems from unauthorized access. We are &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/post-quantum-roadmap\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;targeting 2029&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; for Cloudflare to be fully post-quantum secure.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;ML-DSA, the best all-around post-quantum signature scheme standardized today, has its downsides: itâs much larger on the wire, and many &lt;a href=\&quot;https://github.com/fancy-cryptography/fancy-cryptography\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;tricks&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; we were able to perform with RSA and ECC simply cannot be done with ML-DSA. There are better post-quantum signature schemes on the horizon: last month, NIST &lt;a href=\&quot;https://groups.google.com/a/list.nist.gov/g/pqc-forum/c/LXoTAe5AN78/m/ZXgBCNlgDAAJ\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;announced&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; that it is advancing nine post-quantum signature schemes to the third round of the â&lt;a href=\&quot;https://csrc.nist.gov/projects/pqc-dig-sig\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;signatures on-ramp&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;â. And a draft standard for &lt;a href=\&quot;https://falcon-sign.info/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;FN-DSA&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; (nÃ©e Falcon), which was picked from the previous competition, is expected imminently.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;We have been very interested in advances in post-quantum signature algorithms, and wrote about the progress in &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/sizing-up-post-quantum-signatures/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;2021&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/nist-post-quantum-surprise/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;2022&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/another-look-at-pq-signatures/\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;2024&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, and &lt;a href=\&quot;https://blog.cloudflare.com/pq-2025/#signatures-on-the-horizon\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;2025&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. In this blog post weâll treat you to the latest developments in great detail.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;But first we have to deal with the elephant in the room: These new signature algorithms will not be ready in time for the PQ transition â not even close, as we &lt;a href=\&quot;#timelines\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;will see later on&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt;. The problem is arriving too soon for us to wait. ML-DSA is available today, and it will have to do for the first migration. As Eric Rescorla &lt;a href=\&quot;https://educatedguesswork.org/posts/pq-emergency/\&quot; target=\&quot;_blank\&quot; rel=\&quot;noopener noreferrer\&quot;&gt;&lt;u&gt;wrote&lt;/u&gt;&lt;/a&gt; in 2024:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;blockquote&gt;&lt;p&gt;&lt;i&gt;You go to war with the algorithms you have, not the ones you wish you had.&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/blockquote&gt;&lt;p&gt;Nonetheless, the search for better post-quantum signature algorithms is crucial for several reasons, and we firmly believe it is still the best use of NISTâs limited resources.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Letâs have a look at the signature algorithms in detail. After that weâll look at the timeline for their availability, and the reasons why we still need them.&lt;/p&gt;\n    &lt;div class=\&quot;flex anchor relative\&quot;&gt;\n      &lt;h2 id=\&quot;the-signature-algorithms\&quot;&gt;The signature algorithms&lt;/h2&gt;\n      &lt;a href=\&quot;#the-signature-algorithms\&quot; aria-hidden=\&quot;true\&quot; class=\&quot;relative sm:absolute sm:-start-5\&quot;&gt;\n        &lt;svg width=\&quot;16\&quot; height=\&quot;16\&quot; viewBox=\&quot;0 0 24 24\&quot;&gt;&lt;path fill=\&quot;currentcolor\&quot; d=\&quot;m12.11 15.39-3.88 3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1-3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0-3.5l3.88-3.88a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-3.88 3.89a4.48 4.48 0 0 0 6.33 6.33l3.89-3.88a1 1 0 1 0-1.42-1.42Zm8.58-12.08a4.49 4.49 0 0 0-6.33 0l-3.89 3.88a1 1 0 0 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.88a2.52 2.52 0 0 1 3.5 0 2.47 2.47 0 0 1 0 3.5l-3.88 3.88a1 1 0 1 0 1.42 1.42l3.88-3.89a4.49 4.49 0 0 0 0-6.33ZM8.83 15.17a1 1 0 0 0 1.1.22 1 1 0 0 0 .32-.22l4.92-4.92a1 1 0 0 0-1.42-1.42l-4.92 4.92a1 1 0 0 0 0 1.42Z\&quot;&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;/svg&gt;\n      &lt;/a&gt;\n    &lt;/div&gt;\n    &lt;p&gt;In the table below, we compare the candidate signature algorithms that progressed to the third round (marked by ð¤), with classical algorithms vulnerable to quantum attack (marked by â), and the post-quantum algorithms that are already standardized ( â) or soon will be (ð). Each candidate proposes several variants. We list the most relevant variants to TLS, the protocol used to secure connections on the Internet.                &lt;span style=\&quot;background-color:transparent;color:#FFF;\&quot;&gt;&lt;span                  &lt;span style=\&quot;background-color:transparent;color:#000;\&quot;&gt;&lt;span               &lt;span style=\&quot;background-color:transparent;color:#000;\&quot;&gt;&lt;span               &lt;span style=\&quot;background-color:transparent;color:#000;\&quot;&gt;&lt;span
